`qp_lambda_h(a, active_idx)`:
```
def qp_lambda_h(a, active_idx):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); r=len(active_idx)
    if r==0: return None
    A=np.flip(a)
    M=np.zeros((n,r), dtype=np.float64)
    for j,k in enumerate(active_idx):
       d=n-1-k
       if d>=0:
          L=n-d
          M[d:,j]=A[:L]
       else:
          L=n+d
          M[:L,j]=A[-d:]
    # weighted variance Q with weights a
    # Q = M^T diag(a) M - outer(ms,ms)
    # compute M*a for each column:
    Maw = M * a[:,None]
    ms = Maw.sum(axis=0)
    Q = M.T @ Maw
    Q = Q - np.outer(ms, ms)
    # sym
    Q = 0.5*(Q+Q.T)
    lam=np.full(r,1.0/r)
    # FW 80
    for it in range(80):
       g=2.0*Q.dot(lam)
       jmin=int(np.argmin(g))
       gamma=2.0/(it+2)
       lam = (1.0-gamma)*lam
       lam[jmin] += gamma
       # due round normalize
       s=lam.sum(); if s>0: lam/=s
    h=M.dot(lam)
    return h  # and lam? only h
```
Weighted variance uses weights a sum1. If a_i zeros, those coordinates no variance. Good. Q may negative small; if all zero, h maybe? FW with Q near0 g all same, lam uniform.
Potential expensive: Maw = M * a[:,None] creates n×r (100k*100). Fine. `M.T @ Maw` r×r.

Could we avoid M if r large? r max64. Good.

`qp_refine(a, deadline, tag, verbose)`:
Pseudo:
```
def qp_refine(a, deadline, tag, verbose=False):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_seq(a); best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a)
    if verbose: print
    eta=0.1
    stagnant=0
    active_sizes=[4,8,16,32,64,128]
    phase=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and phase<30:
        r=active_sizes[phase % len]
        conv=fast_self_conv(a)
        sc=2*n*max(conv)
        if sc<best_sc: update
        # choose active: top r but also threshold
        idx=np.argpartition(conv, -r)[-r:]
        idx=np.sort(idx)
        h=qp_lambda_h(a, idx)
        if h is None: phase+=1; continue
        mean_h=float(np.dot(a,h))
        hc=h-mean_h
        scale=float(np.max(np.abs(hc)))
        if scale<1e-14:
           stagnant+=2; eta*=0.5; stage+=1; continue
        hc=hc/scale
        hc=np.clip(hc,-10,10)
        # eta candidates around eta
        etas=[eta*0.125,eta*0.25,eta*0.5,eta,eta*2,eta*4,eta*8]
        best_step=None
        la=np.log(a+1e-300)
        for e in etas:
           if e<=1e-5: continue
           nl=la - e*hc
           nl=nl-np.max(nl)
           na=np.exp(nl); s=na.sum(); na/=s
           sc2=score_norm(na)
           if sc2<best_sc-1e-14:
              if best_step is None or sc2<best_step_sc:
                  best_step=na; best_step_sc=sc2; best_step_eta=e
        if best_step is not None:
           a=normalize_seq(best_step); best_sc=best_step_sc; best=a.copy(); eta=best_step_eta; stagnant=0
           if verbose and ... print
        else:
           stagnant+=1; eta*=0.7
           if stagnant>8: 
              # perturb
              a=normalize_seq(perturb(a, rng, strength=0.1)) ??? Need rng passed.
              stagnant=0
        phase+=1
    return best_sc,best
```

def multiplicative_step(a, h_centered, eta):
   la=np.log(a+1e-300)
   ny=la - eta*h_centered
   ny=ny-np.max(ny)
   ny=np.clip(ny,-700,0)
   b=np.exp(ny)
   b=np.where(b>1e-18/len(b), b, 1e-18/len(b))
   s=b.sum(); b/=s; return b

`choose_active(conv, cap, eps)`:
```
def choose_active(conv, cap, eps):
    cmax=float(np.max(conv))
    thr=cmax - max(1e-14, eps*cmax)
    idx=np.where(conv>=thr)[0]
    if idx.size>cap:
       idx=np.argpartition(conv, -cap)[-cap:]
    if idx.size<1: idx=np.argmax
    else idx=np.sort(idx)
    return idx.astype(int)
```
No nested.

def lse_lbfgs(a, beta, deadline, maxfun):
   a=normalize_seq(a)
   x0=np.log(a)
   opts = {"maxiter": max(20,maxfun//2), "maxfun": int(maxfun), "maxls": 30, "ftol":1e-15,"gtol":1e-12}
   try: res=minimize(lse_objective, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, args=(beta,), options=opts)
   except Exception: res=None
   a2=softmax_a(res.x) if res is not None else a
   sc=score_norm(a2)
   if not finite or sc > score_norm(a)+10: a2=a; sc=score_norm(a)
   return sc,a2
```

Let's write `lse_multi_refine(a, deadline, tag)`:
```
def lse_multi_refine(a, deadline, tag):
    best=a; best_sc=score_norm(a)
    betas=[2000,5000,10000,30000,80000]
    for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem<60: break
       # allocate up to 40% rem but cap 200
       maxfun=int(min(200, max(30, rem*0.35/0.15)))
       sc,a2=lse_lbfgs(best,beta,deadline,maxfun)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc-1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best=a2.copy(); print
       elif sc<best_sc+1e-9:
           # maybe keep a2 if improved micro?
           pass
    return best_sc,best
```

`qp_refine` details code:
```
def qp_refine(a, deadline, rng, tag, verbose=False):
    n=len(a)
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a)
    if verbose: print
    sizes=[8,16,32,64,128,256]
    eps_list=[1e-5,1e-4,1e-3,5e-3,1e-2,2e-2]
    eta=0.05
    stag=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if stag>12:
          # perturb
          a=normalize_seq(perturb(a, rng, strength=0.15))
          stag=0
          # if score worse, okay for diversity
       phase=it
       r=sizes[phase % len(sizes)]
       eps=eps_list[(phase//len(sizes)) % len(eps_list)]
       if r>256: r=256
       conv=fast_self_conv(a)
       sc=2.0*n*float(np.max(conv))
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=a.copy()
       active=choose_active(conv, r, eps)
       h=qp_lambda_h(a, active)
       if h is None:
          it+=1; continue
       # scale h
       hmin=float(np.min(h))
       hstep=h-hmin
       scale=float(np.max(hstep))
       if not finite or scale<1e-15:
          stag+=2; eta*=0.6; it+=1; continue
       hstep=hstep/scale
       # Try steps
       etas=[eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2.0, eta*4.0]
       la=np.log(a+1e-300)
       improved=False
       best_na=None; best_na_sc=sc; best_eta=eta
       for e in etas:
          if e<=0: continue
          ny=la - e*hstep
          ny=ny-np.max(ny)
          ny=np.clip(ny, -700, 0)
          nb=np.exp(ny)
          nb=np.where(nb>1e-18/n, nb, 1e-18/n)
          sns=nb.sum(); nb/=sns
          sc2=score_norm(nb)
          if sc2 < best_na_sc - 1e-14:
              best_na_sc=sc2; best_na=nb; best_eta=e; improved=True
       if improved:
          a=best_na
          if best_na_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best_sc=best_na_sc; best=a.copy()
          eta=best_eta
          stag=0
          if verbose and it%20==0: print
       else:
          stag+=1
          eta*=0.75
          if eta<1e-5: eta=0.05
       it+=1
    return best_sc,best
```

```
def choose_active(conv, cap, eps):
   cmax=conv.max()
   thr=cmax - max(1e-15, eps*cmax)
   idx=np.where(conv>=thr)[0]
   if idx.size > cap:
      idx=np.argpartition(conv, -cap)[-cap:]
   elif idx.size ==0:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(conv))])
   idx=np.sort(idx.astype(int))
   return idx
```

Time for QP: `M` zero n×r. For k active, d. Need ensure slice lengths:
A length n. For d>=0 (k≤n-1): L = n-d = k+1. M[d:n, j] = A[:L]. This gives i=d..n-1, A[i-d]? Let's test n=5, k=2, d=2. Need h_i=a[2-i] for i=0..2: i0 a2, i1 a1, i2 a0, i3/4 0. A=a[::-1]=[a4,a3,a2,a1,a0]. A[:3]=[a2,a1,a0]. M[2:]=A[:3] => M[2]=a2 (i=2), M[3]=a1 (i=3), M[4]=a0 (i=4), wrong! Our derivation of slice was off. Let's fix.
We want M[i,k]=a[k-i] valid. A=a[::-1], A[t]=a[n-1-t]. Need a[k-i] = A[n-1-(k-i)] = A[n-1-k+i] = A[i + (n-1-k)] = A[i+d]. Thus for i such that 0≤i+d<n => i ∈ [0, n-d-1] if d≥0. Then M[i,j] = A[i+d] for i=0..n-d-1. So if d>=0: M[:n-d,j] = A[d:]. For k=2,d=2,n=5: L=3, A[2:5]=[a2,a1,a0], assign M[0:3]=[a2,a1,a0]. correct.
If d<0: need i+d >=0 => i>=-d. M[i,j]=A[i+d] for i=-d..n-1, length n+d. So M[-d:, j] = A[:n+d]. Test k=n (d=-1), need i=1..n-1: M[1:,]=A[:n-1] (a[n-1]..a1). correct. k=2n-2, d=-n+1, L=1, M[n-1:]=A[:1]=a[n-1], index k-i=2n-2-(n-1)=n-1. correct.
Thus:
```
d = n-1-k
if d>=0:
  L=n-d
  M[:L,j] = A[d:]
else:
  L=n+d
  M[-L:,j] = A[:L]
```

def score_norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a); s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return 1e100
   b=fast_self_conv(a); m=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0
   return float(2*n*m/(s*s))
```
Scale invariant, so even if sum not1. Good.

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
   a=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a,float).ravel(), nan=0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0)
   a=np.clip(a,0,None)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return np.full(a.size,1.0/max(1,a.size))
   a=a/s
   # floor
   floor=1e-18/max(1,a.size)
   a=np.where(a>floor,a,floor)
   a=a/a.sum()
   return a
```
If a contains NaNs from FFT? ok.

`resample_sequence`: If input size m, output size n. Use np.interp. If all zeros fallback uniform. Then normalize.
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
   a=...; m=len(a)
   if m==n: return normalize_seq(a)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=True)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=True)
   a2=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   return normalize_seq(a2)
```
If m=0, uniform.

`perturb`:
```
def perturb(a,rng,strength=0.1):
   a=normalize_seq(a); n=len(a)
   mode=rng.integers(0,6)
   if mode==0: q=a*np.exp(strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
   elif mode==1: t=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3); q=(1-t)*a+t*(1.0/n)
   elif mode==2: x=np.linspace(0,1,n); q=a*(1+strength*(2*x-1)*rng.uniform(-1,1))
   elif mode==3:
      # smooth noise: choose k
      k=int(rng.integers(5,min(101,max(7,n//1000))))
      if k%2==0:k+=1
      sig=max(1.0,k/4)
      kern=...; noise=convolve; q=a*np.exp(0.3*strength*noise)
   elif mode==4: q=a*np.exp(0.5*strength*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.uniform(1,10)+2*np.pi*x))
   else: q=a*rng.uniform(0.8,1.2,size=n)
   return normalize_seq(q)
```
No nested.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

Strategy:
- Use FFT-based evaluations for fast score/constraint updates.
- Start from the previous parent when available, plus diversified smooth starts.
- Core local search: active-set exponentiated gradient where the active weights
  are chosen by a small quadratic program aiming to flatten the conjugate
  response h_i = sum_k lambda_k a_{k-i} on the current support.
- Polish with smooth log-sum-exp L-BFGS-B in a softmax parameterization, which
  preserves non-negativity and normalization.
- Use a coarse-to-fine stage and perturb-and-restart stages to avoid local basins.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Global deadline used by callable objectives to abort gracefully.
g_deadline = 1e300


def npop2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = npop2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """Return h_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i}, for i=0..n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = npop2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(max(1, a.size), 1.0 / float(max(1, a.size)), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    n = int(a.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / max(1, n)
    a = np.where(a > floor, a, floor)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    return a


def score_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s < 1e-300:
        return 1e100
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 2.0 * n
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return 1e100
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return normalize_seq(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, endpoint=True)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=True)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)
    return normalize_seq(b)


def add_init(inits, cap, arr, n):
    if inits is None or len(inits) >= max(1, int(cap)):
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != n:
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if arr.sum() < 1e-300:
        arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    inits.append(normalize_seq(arr))


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, cap=24):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_init(inits, cap, p, n)
        add_init(inits, cap, p[::-1], n)

    add_init(inits, cap, np.ones(n), n)

    # Linear tilts.
    for c in np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, 13):
        y = 1.0 + c * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Quadratic tilts.
    for c in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        y = 1.0 + c * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
        add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Cuartics / powers.
    for p in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        y = (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)) ** p + 0.05
        add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Cosine modulations, not all nonnegative before clipping.
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.2):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5):
                y = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + 2.0 * np.pi * ph)
                add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Exponential tapers.
    for rate in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0):
        add_init(inits, cap, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.02, n)
        add_init(inits, cap, np.exp(-rate * np.abs(x - 0.5)) + 0.02, n)

    # Bimodal bumps.
    for width in (0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18):
        for center in (0.2, 0.3, 0.35):
            y = np.exp(-(((x - center) / width) ** 2))
            y += np.exp(-(((x - (1.0 - center)) / width) ** 2))
            y += 0.02
            add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Random smooth low-frequency fields.
    Nf = npop2(max(1, n))
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
    for _ in range(6):
        gamma = float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.8))
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** gamma)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        y = 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.1)) * noise
        add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    # Random multiplicative fields.
    for _ in range(4):
        y = 0.25 + 0.75 * rng.random(n)
        add_init(inits, cap, y, n)

    return inits


def choose_active(conv, cap, eps):
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(conv.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    cap = int(max(1, min(cap, n)))
    cmax = float(np.max(conv))
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        idx = np.argmax(conv).astype(int)
        return np.array([idx], dtype=int)
    thr = cmax - max(1e-15, float(eps) * cmax)
    idx = np.where(conv >= thr)[0]
    if idx.size > cap:
        idx = np.argpartition(conv, -cap)[-cap:]
    elif idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(conv))], dtype=int)
    idx = np.sort(idx.astype(int))
    return idx


def qp_lambda_h(a, active_idx):
    """
    For active convolution indices active_idx, choose lambda >= 0, sum=1,
    to reduce the weighted variance of h_i = sum_lambda a_{k-i} with weights a.
    Returns h (nonnegative).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int).ravel()
    r = int(active_idx.size)
    if n == 0 or r == 0:
        return None

    A = a[::-1]
    M = np.zeros((n, r), dtype=np.float64)

    for j, k in enumerate(active_idx):
        k = int(k)
        if k < 0:
            k = 0
        elif k > 2 * n - 2:
            k = 2 * n - 2
        d = n - 1 - k
        if d >= 0:
            L = n - d
            if L > 0:
                M[:L, j] = A[d:]
        else:
            L = n + d
            if L > 0:
                M[-L:, j] = A[:L]

    if r == 1:
        h = M[:, 0]
        if h.max() <= 0.0:
            return None
        return h

    # Weighted variance: Q = M^T diag(a) M - outer(ms, ms), a sums to 1.
    Maw = M * a.reshape(-1, 1)
    ms = Maw.sum(axis=0)
    Q = M.T @ Maw
    Q = Q - np.outer(ms, ms)
    Q = 0.5 * (Q + Q.T)
    Q = Q + 1e-14 * np.eye(r, dtype=np.float64)

    lam = np.full(r, 1.0 / float(r), dtype=np.float64)

    # Frank-Wolfe for convex quadratic over simplex (small r).
    for it in range(60):
        g = 2.0 * (Q @ lam)
        jmin = int(np.argmin(g))
        gamma = 2.0 / float(it + 2)
        lam = (1.0 - gamma) * lam
        lam[jmin] += gamma
        s = float(lam.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            lam /= s
        else:
            lam[:] = 1.0 / float(r)

    h = M @ lam
    if h.size == 0 or h.max() <= 0.0:
        return None
    return h


def multiplicative_step(a, h_step, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h_step = np.asarray(h_step, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or h_step.size != a.size or eta <= 0.0:
        return a
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    ny = la - float(eta) * h_step
    ny = ny - float(np.max(ny))
    ny = np.clip(ny, -700.0, 0.0)
    b = np.exp(ny)
    n = int(b.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / max(1, n)
    b = np.where(b > floor, b, floor)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a
    b = b / s
    return b


def perturb(a, rng, strength=None):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.22))
    strength = float(np.clip(strength, 0.0, 2.0))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 8))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.6 * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
    elif mode == 3:
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.6 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    elif mode == 4:
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, min(201, n // 100))))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.5)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        q = a * np.exp(0.35 * strength * noise)
    elif mode == 5:
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 11))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph))
    elif mode == 6:
        q = a * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15, size=n)
    else:
        # Reverse plus smooth noise; reversal alone has the same score, but with
        # noise it can cross to another basin after comparison.
        r = a[::-1].copy()
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, min(101, n // 500))))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.5)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        noise = np.convolve(rng.standard_normal(n), kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        q = r * np.exp(0.25 * strength * noise)

    return normalize_seq(q)


def qp_refine(a, deadline, rng, tag="qp", verbose=False):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_seq(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if verbose:
        print("[{}] initial score={:.9f} n={}".format(tag, best_sc, n))

    eta = 0.08
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0

    sizes = [8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 192]
    eps_list = [1e-5, 5e-5, 2e-4, 1e-3, 5e-3, 2e-2]

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if stagnant > 14:
            a = perturb(a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.28)))
            stagnant = 0
            if verbose and it % 50 == 0:
                print("[{} {} perturbed at it={}] score={:.9f}".format(tag, "?", it, score_seq(a)))

        r = int(sizes[it % len(sizes)])
        eps = float(eps_list[(it // len(sizes)) % len(eps_list)])

        conv = fast_self_conv(a)
        sc_now = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv) if conv.size else 0.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc_now) and sc_now < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_now)
            best_a = a.copy()

        active = choose_active(conv, r, eps)
        if active.size == 0:
            stagnant += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        h = qp_lambda_h(a, active)
        if h is None or h.size != n:
            stagnant += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        hmin = float(np.min(h))
        hstep = h - hmin
        scale = float(np.max(hstep))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
            # As a fallback, uniform active response.
            hstep = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            A = a[::-1]
            for k in active:
                k = int(k)
                d = n - 1 - k
                if d >= 0:
                    L = n - d
                    if L > 0:
                        hstep[:L] += A[d:]
                else:
                    L = n + d
                    if L > 0:
                        hstep[-L:] += A[:L]
            if active.size > 0:
                hstep /= float(active.size)
            hmin2 = float(np.min(hstep))
            hstep = hstep - hmin2
            scale = float(np.max(hstep))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
                stagnant += 2
                eta *= 0.6
                if eta < 1e-5:
                    eta = 0.05
                it += 1
                continue
            hstep = hstep / scale

        hstep = hstep / max(1e-15, scale)
        hstep = np.clip(hstep, 0.0, 20.0)

        la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        etas = [eta * 0.5, eta, eta * 2.0, eta * 4.0]
        best_candidate = None
        best_candidate_sc = float(sc_now)
        best_eta = eta
        improved = False

        for e in etas:
            if e <= 1e-8:
                continue
            cand = multiplicative_step(a, hstep, e)
            if cand is None or cand.size != n:
                continue
            sc2 = score_seq(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_candidate_sc - 1e-14:
                best_candidate_sc = float(sc2)
                best_candidate = cand
                best_eta = float(e)
                improved = True

        if improved and best_candidate is not None:
            a = best_candidate
            eta = max(1e-5, min(50.0, best_eta * 1.05))
            stagnant = 0
            if best_candidate_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(best_candidate_sc)
                best_a = a.copy()
                if verbose and it % 20 == 0:
                    print("[{} it={}] score={:.9f} r={} eps={:.2e} eta={:.3e}".format(
                        tag, it, best_sc, r, eps, eta))
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            eta *= 0.75
            if eta < 1e-5:
                eta = 0.05

        it += 1

    if verbose:
        print("[{} final] score={:.9f} time={:.1f}".format(tag, best_sc, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def softmax_a(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.size, 1.0 / max(1, x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def lse_objective(x, beta):
    if time.monotonic() > g_deadline:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    a = softmax_a(x)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        se = 1.0
        e = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        e[0] = 1.0

    w = e / se
    lse = cmax + math.log(se) / beta
    val = 2.0 * float(n) * lse

    wd = float(np.dot(w, c))
    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - wd)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    return float(val), grad


def lse_lbfgs(a, beta, maxfun):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    x0 = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300)).astype(np.float64)
    maxfun = int(max(20, min(250, maxfun)))
    opts = {
        "maxiter": max(20, maxfun // 2),
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "maxls": 30,
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "disp": False,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(lse_objective, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, args=(float(beta),), options=opts)
        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            a2 = normalize_seq(softmax_a(res.x))
        else:
            a2 = a
    except Exception:
        a2 = a

    sc0 = score_seq(a)
    sc2 = score_seq(a2)
    if not np.isfinite(sc2) or sc2 > sc0 + 1e-6:
        a2 = a
        sc2 = sc0
    return float(sc2), a2


def lse_multi_refine(a, deadline, tag="lse"):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_seq(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    print("[{}] initial score={:.9f}".format(tag, best_sc))

    betas = [2000.0, 6000.0, 15000.0, 40000.0, 100000.0]
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 20.0:
            break
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(max(40, min(140, rem * 0.25 / 0.20)))
        sc, a2 = lse_lbfgs(best_a, beta, maxfun)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print("[{} beta={:.0f}] improved to {:.9f}".format(tag, beta, best_sc))
        else:
            # If L-BFGS wandered but not better, do not discard; keep best.
            pass

    print("[{}] final score={:.9f} time={:.1f}".format(tag, best_sc, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def evaluate_starts(n, rng, parent, inits_cap=24):
    inits = generate_inits(int(n), rng, parent, cap=int(inits_cap))
    if not inits:
        return 1e100, np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64) / int(n)
    best_a = inits[0]
    best_sc = score_seq(best_a)
    for arr in inits[1:]:
        sc = score_seq(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = arr.copy()
    print("Best initial score for n={}: {:.9f}".format(n, best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global g_deadline

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(120, budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 50.0
    g_deadline = deadline

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Main optimization length. 16384 is large enough for finite-n improvements
    # but still safe for the direct convolution evaluator.
    n_main = 16384
    if parent is not None:
        ps = int(parent.size)
        if ps > 4096 and ps <= 20000:
            n_main = int(min(max(ps, 10240), 20000))
            # Snap to a nicely sized length; avoid >20000.
            if n_main > 18000:
                n_main = 16384

    # Initial main candidate.
    if parent is not None:
        a_main = resample_sequence(parent, n_main)
        init_sc, a_main_best = evaluate_starts(n_main, rng, parent, cap=18)
        if init_sc < score_seq(a_main):
            a_main = a_main_best
    else:
        a_main = np.ones(n_main, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_main)
        init_sc, a_main_best = evaluate_starts(n_main, rng, parent, cap=28)
        a_main = a_main_best

    best_sc = score_seq(a_main)
    best_a = a_main.copy()
    print("Main initial best: n={}, score={:.9f}".format(n_main, best_sc))

    # Coarse exploration.
    coarse_dead = t_start + max(80.0, min(320.0, 0.14 * total_budget))
    n_coarse = 2048
    if parent is not None:
        a_c = resample_sequence(parent, n_coarse)
    else:
        a_c = np.ones(n_coarse, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_coarse)
    sc_c_init, a_c_ref = evaluate_starts(n_coarse, rng, parent, cap=14)
    if sc_c_init < score_seq(a_c):
        a_c = a_c_ref
    if time.monotonic() < coarse_dead:
        sc_c, a_c = qp_refine(a_c, min(coarse_dead, time.monotonic() + 260.0), rng, tag="coarse", verbose=True)
        a_up = resample_sequence(a_c, n_main)
        sc_up = score_seq(a_up)
        if np.isfinite(sc_up) and sc_up < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_up)
            best_a = a_up.copy()
            print("Coarse upscaled improved best: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Main QP/exponentiated-gradient refinement.
    main_dead = t_start + 0.62 * total_budget
    if time.monotonic() < main_dead:
        sc, a = qp_refine(best_a, main_dead, rng, tag="main", verbose=True)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("Main QP improved best: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # LSE-LBFGS refinement.
    lse_dead = t_start + 0.82 * total_budget
    if time.monotonic() < lse_dead:
        sc, a = lse_multi_refine(best_a, lse_dead, tag="lse")
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("LSE improved best: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Restart diversification.
    restart_dead = deadline - 80.0
    for ri in range(5):
        if time.monotonic() >= restart_dead:
            break
        rem = restart_dead - time.monotonic()
        chunk = max(18.0, min(80.0, rem * 0.55))
        p = perturb(best_a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.18)))
        sc, a = qp_refine(p, time.monotonic() + chunk, rng, tag="restart{}".format(ri), verbose=False)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("Restart {} improved: {:.9f}".format(ri + 1, best_sc))

    # Final short polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        sc, a = qp_refine(best_a, min(deadline - 4.0, time.monotonic() + 40.0), rng, tag="final", verbose=False)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("Final QP improved: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # Scale for safety: evaluator clips each coefficient to [0, 1000].
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, None)
    mx = float(out_arr.max()) if out_arr.size else 0.0
    if mx > 0.0 and np.isfinite(mx):
        out_arr = out_arr / mx * 1000.0
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if out_arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64
    else:
        out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]

    print("Internal best: {:.9f}, n={}, time={:.1f}s".format(best_sc, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))

    # Optional local verification; does not affect return except for logging.
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        print("Verified evaluate_sequence: {:.9f}".format(verified))
    except Exception:
        print("Verification skipped.")

    return out
```
